- **opcua-word-to-markdown**: Migrate an existing Word-authored OPC UA companion specification into this repository, whose source of truth is markdown plus a UANodeSet. Covers producing the reference STS from the .docx, filling in manifest.json and profiles.json, rewriting clauses into the markdown dialect, splitting a large document across several markdown files, carrying figures across as Office sources wired to a generator, and proving by diff that nothing was lost. WHEN: the user has a .docx (or an STS XML converted from one) and a mostly empty repository created from the specification template, and asks to migrate, p… ([bostroemc/OPC30050-PackML](https://github.com/bostroemc/OPC30050-PackML/tree/HEAD/skills/opcua-word-to-markdown) · 2 copies)
- **opd-evolver-on-policy-distillation-agent**: OPD-Evolver - On-policy distillation framework for cultivating holistic agent evolvers. Slow-fast co-evolution with four-level memory hierarchy: read, use, write, maintain experience. Outcome-calibrated memory attribution + privileged hindsight distillation. Use when: (1) building self-evolving agents, (2) memory management beyond storage, (3) agent experience learning. Activation: agent evolver, on-policy distillation, memory hierarchy, self-evolution, experience management. ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/general-ml/opd-evolver-on-policy-distillation-agent) · ★4 · 3 copies)

- **opd-tuning**: Tune Relax On-Policy Distillation (OPD/MOPD) runs — teacher SGLang engine knobs, logits-memory limits, student/teacher GPU split, and multimodal caches. Use when a user asks to speed up or debug an OPD run, mentions "OPD", "MOPD", "on-policy distillation", "蒸馏", a teacher engine OOM, teacher timeouts, idle student/teacher GPUs, or any `--teacher-sglang-*` / `--opd-*` flag. Produces a diagnosis report with cited flags, measured evidence, and concrete fixes; applies the edits only when asked. - **ope-rs-code-implementation**: 当用户想要实现 OPE 核心算法、编写实验代码、或做单元测试时自动触发。常见场景：用户说"帮我实现这个 OPE 估计器"、"写 DR 代码"、"单元测试"。本 skill 完成核心算法实现、单元测试、可复现性配置，不负责批量实验执行与数据验证。 ([pdl-openair/OpenAIR](https://github.com/pdl-openair/OpenAIR/tree/HEAD/skills/ope/ope-rs-code-implementation) · ★16)
- **ope-rs-data-validation**: 当用户想要整理 OPE 实验数据、验证数据质量、处理异常值时自动触发。常见场景：用户说"整理 OPE 数据"、"检查异常值"、"数据清洗"、"验证 MSE 跨种子稳定性"。本 skill 完成数据规范化、可重复性检验、异常值处理，不负责代码实现与实验执行。 ([pdl-openair/OpenAIR](https://github.com/pdl-openair/OpenAIR/tree/HEAD/skills/ope/ope-rs-data-validation) · ★16)
- **ope-rs-data-visualization**: 当用户想要清洗 OPE 实验数据、制作可视化图表、准备论文图表时自动触发。常见场景：用户说"整理 OPE 数据"、"画 MSE 对比图"、"制作消融图表"、"做显著性检验"。本 skill 完成数据聚合、统计检验、图表绘制与标注，不负责论文撰写。 ([pdl-openair/OpenAIR](https://github.com/pdl-openair/OpenAIR/tree/HEAD/skills/ope/ope-rs-data-visualization) · ★16)
- **ope-rs-experiment-design**: 当用户想要设计 OPE 实验方案时自动触发。常见场景：用户说"帮我设计 OPE 实验方案"、"设计对比实验"、"确定评估指标"、"制定实验计划"。本 skill 完成实验系统确立、算法方案设计、实验控制与统计规划三件事，不负责文献检索或代码实现。 ([pdl-openair/OpenAIR](https://github.com/pdl-openair/OpenAIR/tree/HEAD/skills/ope/ope-rs-experiment-design) · ★16)
- **ope-rs-experiment-execution**: 当用户想要运行 OPE 实验、批量评估估计器、或管理实验任务时自动触发。常见场景：用户说"跑 OPE 实验"、"执行批量评估"、"批量运行 FQE"。本 skill 完成批量执行、日志记录、失败重试，不负责代码实现与数据清洗。 ([pdl-openair/OpenAIR](https://github.com/pdl-openair/OpenAIR/tree/HEAD/skills/ope/ope-rs-experiment-execution) · ★16)
- **ope-rs-gap-analysis**: 当用户想要分析 OPE/离线 RL 领域的研究空白、识别未解决问题、分析矛盾实证结果、或评估研究想法的创新性与可行性时自动触发。常见场景：用户说"分析这个领域的研究空白"、"识别当前 OPE 研究的未解决问题"、"评估我这个估计器 idea 的可行性"、"为什么这两篇论文 OPE 估计结果矛盾"。本 skill 依赖 ope-rs-literature-filter 的精读产出，不负责新检索或筛选。 ([pdl-openair/OpenAIR](https://github.com/pdl-openair/OpenAIR/tree/HEAD/skills/ope/ope-rs-gap-analysis) · ★16)
